Abstract: Imitation learning has shown increasing promise for autonomous robotic surgery, yet safe deployment remains challenging due to the safety-critical nature of surgical tasks and the complexity and variability of surgical environments. Failure detection is therefore an essential safeguard, but its development remains difficult due to the challenges of scarce failure data, highly variable manipulation dynamics, and the need to balance missed detections against disruptive false alarms. To address these challenges, we introduce FoMo-FD (Flow-Matching World Model for Failure Detection), a failure detection method that learns nominal short-horizon visual dynamics with an action-conditioned flow-matching world model. FoMo-FD scores the inverse-transport nonconformity of observed endpoint latents, enabling window-level detection of visual-action inconsistencies without requiring failure demonstrations. Detection thresholds are obtained by conformal calibration on successful executions, yielding task-specific alarms without assuming future failure types. We evaluate FoMo-FD on four surgically relevant manipulation tasks with twenty failure modes across simulation and real-world experiments using the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK). Results show that FoMo-FD outperforms observation-level anomaly baselines and a prediction-error variant of the same world model, with the wrist-camera view achieving the strongest performance, including a 96.6% failure detection rate (FDR) at a 1.3% false alarm rate (FAR).

</details>


### [231] [Cross-Embodiment Transfer via Behavior-Aligned Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27549)
*Ajay Sridhar,Jensen Gao,Jonathan Yang,Jean Mercat,Suneel Belkhale,Dorsa Sadigh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中使用行为对齐表示（如物体边界框、语言动作、末端执行器轨迹）来促进跨具身（cross-embodiment）迁移。作者构建了基于仿真的基准来评估不同表示在不同具身间迁移的效果，发现末端执行器轨迹特别有效，且表示在大规模先验数据集下更有用，并能利用无动作数据。该方法将真实机器人策略的任务完成进度提升了28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模模仿学习虽然在机器人操作领域取得了进展，但跨不同机器人具身的迁移仍然困难。作者希望通过行为对齐表示来统一跨具身数据，从而提升迁移性能。

Method: 1) 提出假设：具有跨具身不变性且能预测机器人动作的行为对齐表示有助于跨具身数据统一和迁移。2) 构建了基于仿真的基准测试，评估具有多样跨具身数据的新具身迁移性能。3) 比较不同表示（物体边界框、语言运动、末端执行器轨迹）及其融入VLA模型的不同方式。

Result: 研究发现：末端执行器轨迹对迁移特别有益；表示在更大规模先验数据集下通常更有用；表示可用于利用无动作数据；该方法提升了模拟到真实的跨具身迁移效果，使预训练于仿真数据的真实机器人策略的任务完成进度提高28%。

Conclusion: 行为对齐表示（尤其是末端执行器轨迹）是促进VLA模型跨具身迁移的有效手段，能够利用大规模先验数据和无动作数据，并在模拟到真实的迁移中带来显著性能提升。

Abstract: Recent progress in large-scale imitation learning for robot manipulation has been driven by leveraging datasets across a wide range of robot embodiments. However, achieving significant cross-embodiment transfer is often still challenging. In this work, we study the role of using behavior-aligned representations (e.g., object bounding boxes, language motions, end-effector traces of robot motion) in vision-language-action (VLA) models to promote cross-embodiment transfer. We hypothesize that by possessing invariances across embodiments while being predictive of robot actions, these representations can help unify large-scale cross-embodiment data to enhance transfer. To assess our hypothesis, we develop a simulation-based benchmark designed to assess transfer with diverse cross-embodiment data to new embodiments. Using this benchmark, we compare different representations and ways of incorporating them. We identify that end-effector traces can be particularly beneficial for transfer, representations are generally more useful with larger prior datasets, and can be used to benefit from action-free data. We also demonstrate that they can enhance sim-to-real cross-embodiment transfer, improving task completion progress of real robot policies pre-trained on simulation data by 28%. We provide videos of our evaluations at our website: https://ajaysridhar.com/barx/.

</details>


### [232] [A Systems Engineering Framework for Vision-Language-Enabled UAV Triage and Disaster Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27597)
*Swapnil Saha,Bhuvan Rajanasiriyur Jagadeesha,Karishma Patnaik,Neelakshi Majumdar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探索将视觉语言模型（VLM）嵌入无人机-人类协同响应环中的可行性，构建了集自然语言交互、任务级协调、软件在环实现和ICS通信对齐于一体的架构，并进行了初步人为因素评估，结果显示感知工作负荷降低且AI信任度提高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在灾害响应中的应用（如社交媒体监测、行动方案草拟、警报翻译）仅局限于决策支持角色，无法直接转化为团队和机器人资产的协调行动，反而可能增加操作员负担。本研究旨在将VLM从被动咨询工具提升为主动协调代理，嵌入人类-无人机回环中，减轻操作员认知负荷并提升人机协同效率。

Method: 采用基于模型的系统工程（MBSE）方法设计框架，通过用例图和块定义图描述系统角色、内部结构和组件交互。实现三个核心组件：VLM协调代理、无人机任务控制、任务分配器，集成了自然语言交互、任务级协调、软件在环（SIL）实现以及与国际事故指挥系统（ICS）对齐的通信协议，并在集成仿真与控制环境中搭建验证平台。

Result: 7名参与者的人为因素初步评估显示：感知工作负荷（心理需求、努力程度、挫败感）显著降低，AI信任度和通信清晰度获得高评分。系统成功实现了VLM在人类操作员、任务控制逻辑和无人机任务执行之间的通信协调功能。

Conclusion: 通过整合MBSE、软件在环测试和人为因素评估，本研究推动了面向高压力灾害响应场景的可扩展人机自主协同，对航空航天自主性和公共安全领域具有更广泛的启示意义。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision Language Models (VLMs) have created new opportunities for disaster response, where responders must interpret large volumes of sensor data under time pressure. Current VLM applications include social media monitoring for situational awareness, generation of draft action plans, and translation of technical alerts into public-facing messages. While these efforts can accelerate information flow, they remain largely limited to decision-support roles. Such approaches can increase operator burden because humans must still translate outputs into coordinated actions across teams and robotic assets. This study explores the viability of embedding VLMs as coordination agents within the human-UAV loop. The proposed architecture integrates natural language interaction, mission-level task coordination, software-in-the-loop implementation, and communication aligned with the Incident Command System (ICS). Rather than functioning solely as advisory tools, VLMs facilitate communication between human operators, mission control logic, and UAV task execution. The framework was developed using a Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) approach, with use case and block definition diagrams representing system roles, internal structure, and component interactions. Three key elements, the VLM Coordinator Agent, UAV Mission Control, and Task Allocator, were implemented within an integrated simulation and control environment. A preliminary human-factors evaluation with seven participants showed reduced perceived workload across mental demand, effort, and frustration, along with high ratings for AI trust and communication clarity. By integrating MBSE, software-in-the-loop testing, and human-factors evaluation, this work advances scalable human-autonomy teaming for high-stakes disaster response, with broader implications for aerospace autonomy and civil safety.

</details>


### [233] [Arm2Air: Cross-Embodiment Skeleton Transfer for 3D Relay Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27627)
*Dohun Lee,Kyeonghyun Yoo,Seokmin Kim,Byongho Lee,Seungjoo Oh,Hwangnam Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Arm2Air，一种通过跨具身迁移将机器人手臂的避障骨架知识迁移到无人机中继部署任务中的方法，解决了三维城市环境中无人机中继部署需联合考虑视距遮挡、通信范围、高度和三维障碍的难题。该方法利用预训练的Neural MP模型生成的源域骨架预训练Transformer迁移平台，再通过低秩适配（LoRA）在少量目标域数据上适配，实现计算和数据效率双优的中继部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市环境中通信基础设施受损后，无人机中继网络可恢复连接，但城市中继部署面临挑战：需联合考虑视距遮挡、通信范围、高度和三维障碍物，传统规划器计算开销大且难以应对高遮挡场景。已有的机器人手臂避障规划技术已在类似空间推理问题上表现优异，但跨具身（cross-embodiment）迁移到无人机中继部署尚未被探索。

Method: Arm2Air方法包含以下步骤：(1) 从预训练的Neural MP模型中提取源域机器人手臂运动轨迹，转换为有序骨架（ordered skeletons）；(2) 利用这些骨架预训练一个基于Transformer的迁移平台；(3) 使用少量目标域（无人机）数据通过低秩适配（Low-Rank Adaptation, LoRA）将平台适配到无人机中继领域；(4) 迁移得到的骨架初始化中继链，并针对连通性、瓶颈容量、延迟和移动成本进行细化优化。

Result: 在9个未见的密集三维城市地图上，Arm2Air将中端到端规划耗时中位数减少64.9%（相较于最快的传统规划器）。在30张密集城市地图的高遮挡子集上，相较于IMPC-MD：瓶颈容量提升32.6%、容量方差降低74.7%、最大跳距减少13.2%、跳距方差降低75.2%、中继位移减少16.9%。仅用3张目标域训练地图，Arm2Air相较从头训练将中继位置RMSE降低53.6%，仅更新0.134百万参数（对比Scratch和全量微调的1.383百万参数）。

Conclusion: Arm2Air展示了跨具身迁移在三维路径/中继规划中的有效性，实现了计算效率和样本效率的双重提升，并提出了一个更广泛的原则：有序结构先验（ordered structural priors）可以被迁移到异构具身任务中。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) relay networks can restore connectivity after communication infrastructure is damaged. Urban relay placement is difficult because line-of-sight blockage, communication range, altitude, and three-dimensional obstacles must be considered jointly. Arm2Air transfers obstacle-avoidance skeletons from robot arms to UAV relay placement through cross-embodiment transfer. Source-domain robot-arm motions from a pretrained Neural MP model are converted into ordered skeletons that pretrain a transformer-based transfer platform, which is then adapted to the UAV domain using limited target data and Low-Rank Adaptation. The transferred skeleton initializes a relay chain that is refined for connectivity, bottleneck capacity, delay, and movement cost. On nine held-out high-clutter 3D urban maps, Arm2Air reduced median end-to-end planning runtime by 64.9 percent relative to the fastest conventional planner. On the high-obstruction group of a separate 30-map dense urban holdout, it increased bottleneck capacity by 32.6 percent, reduced capacity variance by 74.7 percent, reduced maximum hop distance by 13.2 percent, reduced hop-distance variance by 75.2 percent, and reduced relay displacement by 16.9 percent relative to IMPC-MD. With only three target-domain training maps, Arm2Air reduced relay-position root mean square error by 53.6 percent relative to training from scratch while updating 0.134 million parameters, compared with 1.383 million for Scratch and Full Fine-tuning. These results demonstrate computationally and data-efficient UAV relay placement and suggest a broader principle for transferring ordered structural priors across heterogeneous embodied tasks.

</details>


### [234] [LabEvolver: Training-Free Experience Evolution for Safe and Grounded Wet-Lab Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27690)
*Jingya Wang,Yuyang Gao,Liuzhenghao Lv,Yonghong Tian,Yuyang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LabEvolver是一个无需训练的框架，为安全且接地气的湿实验室智能体赋予基于执行经验的episodic memory，通过内试循环（状态接地感知、在线规划、安全验证）和外演化循环（将完整轨迹蒸馏为可复用的技能、策略和安全经验）耦合实现。在机器人溶液配制任务中，pH调节完成时间降低48.2%，安全闸拦截降低60.0%；在ALFWorld上，20步内累计成功率从ReAct的76.2%提升至91.4%（500个持续任务），验证了超越湿实验室场景的通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湿实验室自动化智能体在执行任务时需要安全且接地气的操作，但现有方法缺乏从执行经验中学习的机制，无法实现闭环的自动化科学发现。作者旨在通过"边做边学"的经验演化路径，让智能体在保证安全的前提下从实际操作中积累可复用的经验。

Method: 提出双循环框架：内试循环负责状态接地感知、在线规划和安全性验证，确保每步操作都基于环境真实状态且符合安全约束；外演化循环将已完成的轨迹蒸馏为技能、策略和安全经验三类可复用经验并存储。框架无需训练，直接通过经验演化提升性能。

Result: 在机器人溶液配制任务中，pH调节完成时间降低48.2%，安全闸拦截次数降低60.0%；在ALFWorld基准上，500个持续任务中20步内累计成功率从ReAct的76.2%提升到91.4%，证明了方法的真实世界可行性和通用性。

Conclusion: 经验驱动的"边做边学"演化是通往闭环自动化科学发现的可行路径。LabEvolver无需训练即可通过执行经验提升安全性和效率，并具备跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: We introduce LabEvolver, a training-free framework that equips safe and grounded wet-lab agents with episodic memory from execution experience. LabEvolver couples a state-grounded inner trial loop for adaptive perception, online planning, and safety validation with an outer evolution loop that distills completed trajectories into reusable skill, strategy, and safety experience. On robotic solution-preparation tasks, LabEvolver demonstrates real-world feasibility, reducing pH-regulation completion time and safety-gate intercepts by 48.2% and 60.0%, respectively. On ALFWorld, it further improves cumulative success rate within 20 steps from 76.2% with ReAct to 91.4% over 500 continual tasks, showing generality beyond wet-lab settings. These results support learn-by-doing experience evolution as a feasible path toward closed-loop automated scientific discovery. The project page is available at https://github.com/AndyGao6186/LabEvolver.

</details>


### [235] [Write-Safe Flow Field Mapping under Ambiguous Onboard Sensing and Localization Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27713)
*Linhao Jin,Qimin Feng,Peter Gunnarson,Qiang Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种地图参考感知的保守融合框架，解决移动机器人流场建图中因观测歧义和定位漂移导致的幽灵结构累积问题，通过预测局部速度场和学习写入安全分数来衰减不确定的地图更新，在合成喷流/横流环境中将幽灵污染降低42%，在真实推力器尾流硬件回放中降低39%同时保留81%地图覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人可从机载传感推断局部流场结构，但局部看似合理的估计写入全局地图时未必安全。相似的流场结构会产生歧义观测，定位漂移导致预测块写错位置，重复的错误注册更新会累积成持久的幽灵结构，因此需要解决这一建图失败模式。

Method: 提出地图参考感知的保守融合框架：模型预测局部速度场和一个学习得到的写入安全分数，该分数持续衰减不确定的地图更新，同时在无可靠地图参考时允许初始化。

Result: 在合成喷流和横流环境中，相比无门控融合，平均幽灵污染降低42%；使用真实压力和光流测量的推力器尾流进行零样本硬件回放，幽灵污染进一步降低39%，同时保留81%的地图覆盖率。

Conclusion: 安全的建图写入对于在歧义感知和定位漂移条件下的流场建图至关重要，实验结果验证了所提框架的有效性。

Abstract: Mobile robots can infer local flow structure from onboard sensing, but a locally plausible estimate is not always safe to write into a global map. Similar flow structures may produce ambiguous observations, while localization drift causes predicted patches to be written at incorrect locations. Repeated misregistered updates then accumulate into persistent ghost structures. We address this failure mode with a map-reference-aware conservative fusion framework. The model predicts a local velocity patch and a learned write-safety score that continuously attenuates uncertain map updates while permitting initialization when no reliable map reference is available. Across synthetic jet and crossflow environments, the proposed method reduces average ghost contamination by 42% relative to ungated fusion. A zero-shot hardware replay using real pressure and optical-flow measurements from a thruster wake further reduces ghost contamination by 39% while retaining 81% map coverage. These results show that safe map writing is critical for flow mapping under ambiguous sensing and localization drift.

</details>


### [236] [RedFlow: Redirect Failure into Action-Level Corrections for Flow-matching VLA Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27782)
*Zhengyang Yan,Junhao Li,Fangqi Zhu,Zijun Wang,Quanxin Shou,Yikun Miao,Xiaoyi Pang,Zicong Hong,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RedFlow是一个细粒度的离线强化学习框架，通过将失败经验重定向为动作级别的校正监督，用于流匹配视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的机器人操作任务，在LIBERO基准和真实世界任务中显著优于现有离线RL基线，并接近在线强策略方法但仅需约十分之一的训练样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配VLA策略在部署中常因分布偏移产生复合误差。离线RL可用于利用 rollout 数据改进策略，但现有方法要么忽略失败数据，要么仅在轨迹级别利用，导致学习效率低且错误持续存在。

Method: RedFlow包含两个核心组件：(1) 上下文感知校正匹配机制（Context-Aware Corrective Matching），识别导致失败的动作并从相似上下文中检索成功替代方案作为校正目标；(2) 自适应重定向目标（Adaptive Redirection Objective），联合强化成功动作、抑制不良动作，并将可恢复的失败重定向至校正目标。

Result: 在LIBERO基准和三个真实世界操作任务上，RedFlow一致优于最先进的离线RL基线，将真实世界成功率从56.7%提升到74.7%，同时匹配PPO、GRPO和DDPO等强在线方法，但训练样本需求约减少一个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过将成功和失败经验转换为密集监督信号，RedFlow实现了从混合质量数据中稳健地进行恢复学习，有效解决了VLA策略部署中的分布偏移和复合误差问题。

Abstract: Flow-matching Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies have shown strong potential for robotic manipulation but often suffer from compounding errors caused by distribution shifts during deployment. While offline reinforcement learning (RL) provides a practical way to improve deployed policies using rollout data, existing methods either ignore failure data or exploit it only at the trajectory level, resulting in low learning efficiency and persistent errors. We propose **RedFlow**, a fine-grained offline RL framework that redirects failure experiences into action-level corrective supervision for flow-matching VLA policies. RedFlow consists of two key components: (1) a **Context-Aware Corrective Matching** mechanism that identifies failure-inducing actions and retrieves successful alternatives from similar contexts as corrective targets, and (2) an **Adaptive Redirection Objective** that jointly reinforces successful actions, suppresses undesirable ones, and redirects recoverable failures toward corrective targets. By converting both successful and failed experiences into dense supervision, RedFlow enables robust recovery learning from mixed-quality data. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark and three real-world manipulation tasks show that RedFlow consistently outperforms state-of-the-art offline RL baselines, improving the real-world success rate from 56.7% to 74.7%. It also matches strong on-policy methods (PPO, GRPO, and DDPO) while requiring roughly an order of magnitude fewer training samples.

</details>


### [237] [DexDirect: Direct Kinesthetic Arm Guidance for Efficient Dexterous Demonstration Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27784)
*Beom Jun Kim,Shiu-Jen Wang,Jonathan Liu,Alvin Zhu,Quanyou Wang,Hanzhang Fang,Feng Xu,Mingzhang Zhu,Yuchen Cui,Dennis W. Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DexDirect，一种直接运动学引导的灵巧操作演示收集接口。操作员直接拖拽6自由度重力补偿机械臂，同时通过单摄像头将另一只手的动作重映射到16关节13自由度的灵巧手上。用户研究显示其成功率分别是纯视觉基线(AnyTeleop)的17.2倍和位姿追踪基线(TeleDex)的3.2倍，NASA-TLX显示其大幅降低心理需求、努力和挫败感。基于DexDirect演示训练的扩散策略在立方体抓取放置任务上达到90%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作演示的规模化收集是机器人学习的主要瓶颈。高保真接口需要昂贵硬件和繁琐设置，而低成本替代品控制精度低且给操作者带来较大认知负担。需要一个性价比高、可扩展且易于使用的演示收集方案。

Method: DexDirect采用双通道方案：操作员通过手柄直接拖拽6自由度重力补偿机械臂进行手臂运动引导（直接运动学引导），同时利用单个网络摄像头实时追踪操作员另一只手并重映射到16关节13自由度的灵巧机器人手部动作。该方法结合了直接触觉引导的精确性和视觉手部重映射的低设置成本。

Result: 用户研究结果表明：相比纯视觉基线(AnyTeleop)收集成功率提高17.2倍，相比位姿追踪基线(TeleDex)提高3.2倍；NASA-TLX评分显示心理需求、努力和挫败感大幅降低（但物理需求有所增加）；基于该方法收集的数据训练的扩散策略在立方体拾放任务上达到90%成功率。

Conclusion: 直接运动学手臂引导结合视觉手部重映射提供了一种高效、低设置成本且可扩展的灵巧操作演示收集接口，在收集效率和操作者体验方面均优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Scalable collection of dexterous manipulation demonstrations remains a major bottleneck for robot learning. High-fidelity interfaces often require costly hardware and extensive setup, while low-setup, low cost alternatives tend to provide less precise control and impose greater cognitive workload on operators. We present DexDirect, a direct kinesthetic arm guidance for efficient dexterous demonstration collection. The operator drags a 6-DoF gravity-compensated robot arm directly by a handle, while a single webcam retargets operator's other hand onto a 16 joints 13-DoF dexterous robot hand. User studies suggest DexDirect collects 17.2x and 3.2x more successful demonstrations compared to purely vision (AnyTeleop) and pose-tracking (TeleDex) baselines. An adapted NASA-TLX shows DexDirect greatly reduces mental demand, effort, and frustration, despite raising physical demand. A diffusion policy trained on DexDirect demonstrations reaches a 90% success rate on a cube pick-and-place task. These results suggest that direct kinesthetic arm guidance combined with vision-based hand retargeting provides an efficient low-setup and scalable interface for collecting dexterous manipulation demonstrations

</details>


### [238] [RoboBRIDGE: A Modular Framework for Bridging Policies to Robust Real-World Robotic Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27881)
*Sihyung Yoon,Minjong Yoo,Sanghyun Ahn,Seojeong Choi,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboBRIDGE是一个模块化框架，通过在预训练VLA模型外围构建由Monitor、Perceptor、Planner、Controller和Robot Interface五个协调模块组成的编排层，将VLA从纯粹的动作预测器系统性地转化为可靠的机器人智能体，在LIBERO、RoboCasa和真实世界场景中均优于独立策略和先前增强方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VLA模型是有效的动作预测器，但直接将其部署为机器人智能体存在三大关键缺口：缺乏故障恢复机制、长时程执行中行为不一致、以及对观测变化、任务或本体变化的鲁棒性有限。现有解决方案仅通过模型重训练或环境特定模块来分别解决这些局限，缺少一个通用框架来系统性转化预训练VLA为完整机器人智能体。

Method: RoboBRIDGE采用模块化架构，提供五模块协调的编排层：(1) Monitor——快速故障检测配合分层恢复机制，在错误级联前进行纠正；(2) Perceptor——当环境偏离当前计划时异步更新场景理解，避免执行阻塞；(3) Planner——负责触发重规划；(4) Controller——通过原始技能微调，将操作分解为域不变的原始技能并配备专用LoRA适配器，降低VLA对域偏移的敏感性；(5) Robot Interface——连接机器人硬件层。该框架可使用现成的预训练VLA组件进行组装。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboCasa基准测试以及涉及多种机器人平台和VLA骨干网络的实际部署案例中，RoboBRIDGE在各项指标上均一致性地优于独立策略和先前增强型VLA部署方案。

Conclusion: 可靠的机器人智能体并非仅通过扩展动作预测器的规模就能实现，而是需要围绕其构建结构化的编排机制。RoboBRIDGE证明了模块化编排层是使VLA模型从动作预测器转向真正机器人智能体的关键路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have attracted growing interest as a scalable approach to robotic manipulation. While these models are effective action predictors, deploying them as robotic agents exposes critical gaps: no mechanism for failure recovery, inconsistent execution over long horizons, and limited robustness to shifts in observations, tasks, or embodiments. Existing solutions address these limitations individually through model retraining or environment-specific modules, yet what is needed is a general framework that systematically transforms a pretrained VLA into a robotic agent. We present RoboBRIDGE, a modular framework that provides an orchestration layer over five coordinated modules, namely Monitor, Perceptor, Planner, Controller, and Robot Interface, to compose robust robotic agents from off-the-shelf components, including pretrained VLAs. The Monitor pairs rapid failure detection with hierarchical recovery to correct errors before they cascade. When the environment diverges from the current plan, the Planner triggers replanning while the Perceptor updates scene understanding asynchronously, avoiding execution stalls. Within the Controller, primitive skill fine-tuning factors manipulation into domain-invariant primitives with dedicated LoRA adapters, reducing sensitivity to domain shifts when a VLA is used. Across LIBERO, RoboCasa, and real-world case studies spanning multiple robot platforms and VLA backbones, RoboBRIDGE consistently outperforms both standalone policies and prior augmented VLA deployments. These results suggest that reliable robotic agency does not arise from scaling action predictors alone, but from structured orchestration around them.

</details>


### [239] [Static In, Dynamic Out: Counterfactual Action Augmentation for Moving Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27890)
*Woo Chul Shin,Zhenyang Chen,Alfred Cueva,Nadun Ranawaka Arachchige,Yingyan Celine Lin,Benjamin Joffe,Shreyas Kousik,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SIDO（静态输入、动态输出）方法，通过反事实动作增强使仅基于静态物体演示训练的策略能够适应测试时未见的物体运动，将移动物体操作分解为"预测物体位置"和"到达预测位姿"两个子问题，在仿真和真实任务中显著提升动态物体操作成功率且不损失静态性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉运动策略在静态目标物体的操作任务上取得了进展，但实际部署中物体常处于运动状态（如传送带上的零件、风中摇摆的水果），违反了静态假设。现有的静态演示训练策略无法应对测试阶段物体运动带来的挑战。

Method: SIDO将移动物体操作分解为两个子问题：预测物体未来位置，以及到达该预测位姿。其核心做法是将物体位移到反事实的未来位置，并调整演示的动作块以保持手-物相对位姿，从而得到目标条件化的策略。部署时由一个物体位姿预测器提供未来位置输入。

Result: 在三个仿真任务（Mug、Square、Stack）的5种物体运动模式以及两个真实任务（Gantry、Peachtree）中，SIDO相比基线方法在移动物体操作上获得更高的成功率，同时保持了静态物体上的性能。

Conclusion: SIDO通过反事实动作增强和手-物相对位姿保持机制，有效地将静态演示策略泛化到动态物体场景，为实际部署中移动物体的机器人操作提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies have advanced on manipulation tasks where the target object stays static during execution, but real deployments break this assumption: parts drift on conveyors and fruits sway in the wind. We introduce Static In, Dynamic Out (SIDO), a counterfactual action augmentation that enables a policy trained only on static object demonstrations to adapt to unseen object motion at test time. Our key idea is to factorize moving object manipulation into two sub-problems: predicting where the object will be, and reaching that predicted pose. SIDO displaces the object to a counterfactual future position and morphs the demonstrated action chunk to preserve the hand-object relative pose, yielding a goal-conditioned policy. At deployment an object pose predictor supplies the future position. Across three simulated tasks (Mug, Square, Stack) under five object motion patterns and two real-world tasks (Gantry, Peachtree), SIDO improves moving object success over the baselines while preserving static object performance. Project website: https://sido-staticindynamicout.github.io/.

</details>


### [240] [Learning Social Robot Navigation By Sensing Human Legs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27922)
*Alberto Vaglio,Andrea Garulli,Antonio Giannitrapani,Renato Quartullo,Tommaso Van Der Meer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CALF（腿部特征的卷积注意力）架构，一种端到端神经网络，直接从2D LiDAR扫描中解读腿部运动并生成安全导航指令，解决传统方法将行人简化为圆形而忽略腿部信息的局限。配套LegNav仿真器（基于JAX）结合2D LiDAR光线追踪和新型行人步态模型，通过深度强化学习训练策略，并在TurtleBot 4上零样本部署验证，单GPU训练时间不足1小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在行人中导航时，低置2D LiDAR传感器主要观测到的是行人的腿部而非完整人体，但现有基于学习的导航方法仍将行人视为简单形状（如圆形），忽略了腿部运动这一关键感知信息，存在环境感知与模型假设之间的差距。

Method: 提出CALF架构，结合卷积层、注意力机制和MLP，直接从LiDAR扫描中解读腿部运动并生成导航指令。采用深度强化学习算法在自研的LegNav仿真器中训练策略；LegNav使用2D LiDAR光线追踪并引入新颖的行人步态模型，用JAX编写以支持高效GPU训练。

Result: CALF策略在导航性能和社会合规性方面优于经典与基于学习的基线方法；通过TurtleBot 4零样本部署的真实世界实验验证，生成的轨迹平滑且社会合规；LegNav仿真器可在单块消费级GPU上于1小时内完成部署就绪策略的训练。

Conclusion: 利用腿部特征而非简单形状建模，结合轻量仿真器和高效训练方案，可以显著提升机器人在行人环境中的导航性能和社交符合度，且训练的模型具备直接部署到真实平台的能力。

Abstract: Robots navigating among pedestrians typically sense their surroundings with a 2D LiDAR mounted close to the ground. At that height, the sensor mostly sees moving legs rather than whole people, yet most learning-based navigation methods still treat pedestrians as simple shapes like circles. This paper addresses that gap with CALF (Convolutional Attention for Leg Features), an end-to-end neural architecture that combines convolutional layers, attention, and MLP to interpret leg motion directly from LiDAR scans and produce safe navigation commands. The CALF policy is trained using deep reinforcement learning algorithms within LegNav, a custom lightweight 2D simulator that combines 2D LiDAR ray tracing with a novel pedestrian gait model. The resulting policy is compared against classical and learning-based baselines in terms of navigation performance and social compliance. The approach is validated through real-world experiments via zero-shot deployment on a TurtleBot 4, yielding smooth and socially compliant trajectories. Written in JAX, the LegNav simulator enables the training of a deployment-ready CALF policy in under an hour on a single consumer GPU.

</details>


### [241] [RaDiVe: Robust 4D Radar Odometry with Distance-Bounded NDT and Velocity-Discrepancy Point Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28045)
*Sangwoo Jung,Dongjae Lee,Chiyun Noh,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RaDiVe，一种4D雷达里程计框架，通过距离受限的NDT配准、基于多普勒速度差异的不确定性加权模型以及基于SDF隐式神经映射的表面点提取，在多个公开数据集上平均将平移ATE降低44.4%、旋转ATE降低21.3%，同时保持实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D雷达在恶劣天气下具有鲁棒感知优势，但其点云固有的稀疏性、噪声大和位置精度有限等特性，给基于配准的里程计带来了显著挑战。现有方法难以在保持实时性的同时取得足够的精度和鲁棒性。

Method: (1) 提出距离受限的NDT（Normal Distributions Transform），通过限制对应搜索仅发生在近距离体素对之间，提升优化稳定性和计算效率；(2) 提出速度差异点不确定性模型，根据每个4D雷达点测量到的多普勒径向速度与由估计自我速度预测的径向速度之间的差异来对每个点进行加权；(3) 引入基于隐式神经映射的SDF（Signed Distance Function）表面点提取，构建几何一致且经过噪声过滤的局部子地图。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上的评估表明，RaDiVe在平移绝对轨迹误差（ATE）上平均优于现有4D雷达里程计基线44.4%，在旋转ATE上平均提升21.3%，并且保持实时性能。

Conclusion: RaDiVe通过结合距离受限NDT配准、多普勒速度差异不确定性加权和SDF隐式神经表面提取，有效解决了4D雷达点云稀疏、噪声大和位置精度受限的问题，显著提升了雷达里程计的精度和鲁棒性，同时满足实时性要求。源代码将开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in 4D radar enable robust perception in adverse weather; however, the inherent sparsity, noise, and limited positional precision of radar point clouds pose significant challenges for registration-based odometry. In this letter, we propose RaDiVe, a 4D radar odometry framework designed to improve the accuracy and robustness of radar point-cloud registration. We introduce a distance-bounded Normal Distributions Transform (NDT), which improves optimization stability and computational efficiency by restricting the correspondence search to near-distance voxel pairs. To mitigate measurement ambiguity, we propose a velocity-discrepancy point uncertainty model that weights each input 4D radar point according to the discrepancy between its measured Doppler radial velocity and the radial velocity predicted from the estimated ego-velocity. Furthermore, we incorporate Signed Distance Function (SDF)-based surface point extraction via implicit neural mapping to construct a geometrically consistent and noise-filtered local submap. Evaluations across multiple public datasets demonstrate that RaDiVe outperforms existing 4D radar odometry baselines by 44.4% in translational Absolute Trajectory Error (ATE) and 21.3% in rotational ATE on average, while maintaining real-time performance. The source code will be made publicly available to the robotics community: https://github.com/to-be-open-sourced.

</details>


### [242] [UniCross: Unified Cross-Skill Dexterous Manipulation Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28198)
*Hui Zhang,Julian Ferchow,Jie Song,Mirko Meboldt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将灵巧操作中的四种关键技能（抓取、重定位、手内旋转、手内平移）建模为单一公式，共享相同的状态空间、动作空间和目标结构，从而支持跨技能策略的蒸馏、泛化、鲁棒性和长时程技能组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的灵巧操作方法将这四种技能分别建模，使用技能特定的动作约束、目标甚至专门的手部形态，破坏了长时间操作所需的技能兼容性和连续性。作者旨在统一这些技能，揭示它们之间内在的一致性。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，将四种灵巧操作技能整合进单一公式，共享相同的状态空间和动作空间以及共同的目标结构，从而蒸馏出一个跨技能策略。

Result: 该统一策略在每种技能上表现强劲，能泛化到未见过的物体，对扰动保持鲁棒，并能无缝衔接多种技能以实现长时间操作。框架也能在不同手部形态间有效迁移。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，不同的灵巧操作技能可以被视为共享任务公式的不同实例，揭示了不同行为之间内在的一致性，为灵巧手的长时程组合操作提供了新视角。

Abstract: Many dexterous manipulation tasks require the object to remain securely held throughout the interaction. From the perspective of hand-object relational motion, such manipulation comprises four canonical skills: grasping, relocation, in-hand rotation, and in-hand translation. Human hands flexibly compose these skills to accomplish complex tasks. Existing approaches, however, model these skills separately with skill-specific action constraints, objectives, or even dedicated hand morphologies, which breaks the compatibility and continuity required for long-horizon composition. In this work, we present a unified framework that models all four skills in a single formulation that shares the same state and action spaces and a common objective structure. This formulation enables straightforward distillation of a single cross-skill policy that performs strongly on every skill, generalizes to unseen objects, stays robust to disturbances, and chains skills seamlessly into long-horizon manipulation. The framework also transfers effectively across different hand morphologies. Overall, our results suggest that different dexterous manipulation skills can be viewed as instantiations of a shared task formulation, revealing the intrinsic consistency across different behaviors.

</details>


### [243] [When Robots Exchange Meaning: A Demo of Goal-Oriented Semantic Communications for Collaborative Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28256)
*Peizheng Li,Xinyi Lin,Sajida Gufran,Adnan Aijaz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个机器人-边缘语义通信（SemCom）测试平台，将机器人端视觉压缩、边缘端语义建图和仪表板交互相结合，展示了从语义视觉传输到对象级地图交互的完整路径，为任务感知的6G网络研究提供实践平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作机器人是任务导向的6G代表性用例，通信质量应体现在任务执行、环境理解和闭环操作上，而非仅关注数据包交付。需要一个将语义通信与具体机器人任务相结合的测试平台来验证这一理念。

Method: 构建机器人-边缘语义通信测试平台：移动机器人搭载RGB-D传感器和LiDAR运行ROS 2，机器人端通过ONNX Runtime编码器将RGB帧压缩为VQ-VAE token；边缘侧使用PyTorch解码器进行重建，并利用Jetson Orin节点执行RTAB-Map建图、语义对象处理和浏览器可视化。320×240图像被表示为80×60的token网格，打包负载5400字节，实现42.67倍压缩。

Result: 实现了42.67X的图像压缩比，重建的视觉流与深度、位姿和3D建图信息关联，生成语义地图供下游机器人应用使用，端到端演示验证了语义通信在机器人任务中的可行性。

Conclusion: 该演示平台展示了从语义视觉传输到对象级地图交互的完整路径，为未来在语义通信、具身AI和物理AI机器人交叉领域的任务感知6G网络研究提供了实用平台。

Abstract: Collaborative robotics is a representative task-oriented 6G use-case, where communication quality should be reflected in mission execution, environment understanding, and closed-loop operation rather than packet delivery alone. This demo paper presents a robot-edge semantic communication (SemCom) testbed integrating robot-side visual compression, edge-side semantic mapping, and dashboard-based mission interaction. A mobile robot equipped with RGB-D sensing and LiDAR runs ROS 2, while a Jetson Orin edge node performs reconstruction, RTAB-Map mapping, semantic object handling, and browserbased visualization. As an initial proof of concept, RGB frames are encoded on the robot into VQ-VAE tokens using an ONNX Runtime encoder and reconstructed on the edge using a PyTorch decoder. A 320 X 240 image is represented by an 80 X 60 token grid with a packed payload of 5400 bytes, corresponding to a 42.67X reduction relative to model-input RGB bytes. The reconstructed visual stream is further associated with depth, pose, and 3D mapping information to generate a semantic map for downstream robotic applications. The demo exposes the full path from semantic visual transport to object-level map interaction, and provides a practical platform for future task-aware 6G networking studies at the intersection of SemCom, embodied AI, and physical AI-enabled robotics. A video of the demo is available at https://tinyurl.com/Tos09

</details>


### [244] [SemAnCorr: Semantic Anchored Correspondence for Zero-Shot Manipulation Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28382)
*Xiaoxiang Dong,William Baron,Hongyi Chen,Uksang Yoo,Jeffrey Ichnowski,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SemAnCorr，一个无需训练的框架，通过联合位姿-对应关系优化选择语义一致的锚点区域，并利用函数映射将约束传播到整个物体表面，从而建立既保持语义一致性又具备几何连贯性的稠密对应关系，实现跨几何差异物体的操作技能迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于稠密视觉描述符和3D特征场的对应关系方法，在特征匹配时往往产生空间上不连贯的对应结果，无法恢复技能迁移所需的局部几何坐标系，导致跨物体实例的技能迁移可靠性受限。

Method: SemAnCorr采用两阶段策略：首先通过联合位姿-对应关系优化选择语义一致的锚点区域，然后利用函数映射（functional maps）将这些锚点约束传播到整个物体表面，从而生成既保留语义一致性又具备几何连贯性的稠密对应。

Result: 在基于PartNet-Mobility构建的稠密对应基准上达到90.8%的语义准确率，几何连贯性优于近期最先进基线；在真实世界实验中，仅需单一演示即可实现显著更可靠的零样本操作技能迁移。

Conclusion: SemAnCorr证明，无需训练即可通过语义锚点选择与函数映射传播相结合来获得高质量稠密对应，从而有效提升跨几何异构物体的操作技能迁移性能，兼顾语义一致性与几何连贯性。

Abstract: Transferring manipulation skills across object instances that share functionality but differ in geometry remains a fundamental challenge in robot learning. While recent correspondence methods leverage dense visual descriptors and 3D feature fields, nearest-neighbor feature matching often produces spatially incoherent correspondences that fail to recover the local geometric frames required for reliable skill transfer. We introduce SemAnCorr, a training-free framework that establishes dense correspondence by selecting semantically consistent anchor regions through joint pose-correspondence optimization and propagating these constraints over the object surface using functional maps. The resulting correspondences preserve both semantic consistency and geometric coherence, enabling object-centric manipulation skills to transfer across geometrically diverse instances. We evaluate SemAnCorr on a dense correspondence benchmark built on PartNet-Mobility, achieving 90.8% semantic accuracy in our benchmark evaluation while improving geometric coherence over recent state-of-the-art baselines. Finally, we show that these improvements translate directly into real-world manipulation performance: using a single demonstration, SemAnCorr enables substantially more reliable zero-shot manipulation skill transfer to previously unseen objects than existing correspondence methods. Videos and additional visualizations are available at [https://semancorr.github.io](https://semancorr.github.io) .

</details>


### [245] [TacWAM: Anchor-Guided World Action Model with Mechanics-Aware Tactile Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28391)
*Lei Jin,Yiding Ma,Xin Zhang,Chen Gao,Wei Wu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacWAM是一种力学感知的触觉世界动作模型，通过空间对齐融合触觉编码器、触觉历史编码器和锚点引导三模态注意力机制，在四个真实世界的接触密集操作任务中达到75.0%的平均成功率，比最强基线高出37.5个百分点，验证了未来触觉监督结合信息丰富的触觉表征和部署一致的信息约束能够提升接触感知的动作学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）主要依赖视觉未来的预测，而视觉预测虽能捕捉场景结构和物体运动，但在接触密集操作中无法为力、形变、剪切和滑动提供充分的监督信号。因此需要设计同时满足两个要求的触觉未来预测：触觉未来应承载有意义的物理信息，且不应成为动作生成的先验特权线索。

Method: TacWAM通过三步解决该挑战：1）空间对齐融合（SAF）触觉编码器将触觉外观、密集力场和形变流映射到共享潜空间，并通过双侧力和力矩重建保留全局接触信息；2）触觉历史编码器提供时间上下文，使未来触觉预测反映力和形变随时间的变化；3）锚点引导三模态（AGT）注意力将当前视觉/触觉锚点、未来预测token和动作token分离，使未来触觉状态可监督训练但不被动作分支直接读取。

Result: 在四个真实世界接触密集操作任务（脆弱抓取、持续表面接触、动态手中操作）上，TacWAM达到75.0%的平均成功率，超过最强基线37.5个百分点。消融实验显示，移除触觉历史和放宽未来预测目标访问均导致性能一致下降。

Conclusion: 未来触觉监督在一定条件下（信息丰富的触觉表征、部署一致的信息约束）可以显著提升接触感知的动作学习能力，为世界动作模型在接触密集操作中的应用提供了有效路径。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) combine future-state prediction with robot action generation, but existing approaches largely rely on visual futures. Visual prediction captures scene structure and object motion, yet provides limited supervision for force, deformation, shear, and slip during contact-rich manipulation. This creates two design requirements: tactile futures should carry meaningful physical information, and they should not become privileged cues for action generation. We present TacWAM, a mechanics-aware tactile WAM that addresses this challenge in three steps. First, a Spatially Aligned Fusion (SAF) Tactile Encoder maps tactile appearance, dense force fields, and deformation flow into a shared latent prediction space, with bilateral force and torque reconstruction preserving global contact information. Second, a tactile history encoder provides temporal context so future tactile prediction reflects how force and deformation change beyond the current tactile observation. Third, Anchor-Guided Tri-Modal (AGT) Attention separates current visual and tactile anchors, future prediction tokens, and action tokens, allowing future tactile states to supervise training without being directly read by the action branch. We evaluate TacWAM on four real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks covering fragile grasping, sustained surface contact, and dynamic in-hand manipulation. TacWAM achieves an average success rate of 75.0%, exceeding the strongest evaluated baseline by 37.5 percentage points. Staged ablations show consistent degradation when tactile history is removed and access to future prediction targets is relaxed. These results indicate that future tactile supervision can improve contact-aware action learning when combined with informative tactile representations and deployment-consistent information constraints.

</details>


### [246] [FasTac: A Curved Multispectral Vision-Based Tactile Sensor for High-Speed High-Precision 3D Shape and Force Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28416)
*Xiaofan Lu,Kaiji Huang,Jiahui Chen,Yuankai Lin,Hua Yang,Zhouping Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FasTac，一种弧形视觉触觉传感器，集成多光谱光度立体视觉、动态卷积力估计（HyperForce）和FPGA硬件加速，实现高精度3D重建、三轴力估计和高速处理（1.09ms），深度MAE降至0.0415mm，法向/剪切力NMAE分别为2.74%和2.39%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弧形视觉触觉传感器难以在紧凑体积内同时实现精确的3D重建、三轴力估计和高速处理，无法满足灵巧操作对精细接触几何、法向/切向载荷区分以及瞬态信号捕捉的需求。

Method: 1) 采用单图像传感器同步多光谱成像，获得空间对齐的观测用于鲁棒表面法向估计，并结合边界先验进行快速泊松深度重建；2) 提出HyperForce，利用位置感知动态卷积对弧形弹性体的空间非均匀力学响应建模，估计三轴力；3) 将完整的图像-法向-力处理流水线部署在FPGA上实现硬件加速。

Result: 实验结果表明：近红外照明和边界先验将深度平均绝对误差从0.2730mm降至0.0415mm；HyperForce对法向力和剪切力的归一化平均绝对误差分别为2.74%和2.39%；FPGA部署将处理延迟从GPU的3.26ms缩短至1.09ms。多目标重建、反馈抓取和振动测量验证了细粒度几何感知、稳定力反馈和动态接触感知能力。

Conclusion: FasTac通过多光谱光度立体视觉、动态卷积力估计与FPGA硬件加速的协同设计，在弧形触觉传感领域同时实现了高精度3D重建、三轴力估计和低延迟处理，为灵巧操作提供了综合性解决方案。

Abstract: Curved tactile fingertips for dexterous manipulation must resolve fine contact geometry, distinguish normal and tangential loads, and capture transient signals. Existing curved vision-based tactile sensors struggle to combine accurate 3D reconstruction, three-axis force estimation, and high-speed processing in a compact form. This article presents FasTac, a curved vision-based tactile sensor integrating multispectral photometric stereo, dynamic-convolution force estimation, and hardware acceleration on a field-programmable gate array (FPGA). Single-image-sensor simultaneous multispectral imaging provides spatially aligned observations for robust surface normal estimation, followed by boundary-prior fast Poisson depth reconstruction. HyperForce uses position-aware dynamic convolution to model the spatially nonuniform mechanical response of curved elastomers and estimate three-axis forces. The complete image-to-normal-force pipeline is deployed on an FPGA. Experiments show that near-infrared (NIR) illumination and the boundary prior decrease depth mean absolute error (MAE) from 0.2730 mm to 0.0415 mm; HyperForce achieves normalized mean absolute error (NMAE) values of 2.74% and 2.39% for normal and shear forces, respectively; and FPGA deployment shortens processing latency from 3.26 ms on the GPU to 1.09 ms. Multi-object reconstruction, feedback grasping, and vibration measurement validate fine geometric perception, stable force feedback, and dynamic contact sensing.

</details>


### [247] [One Future, Every Robot: Label-Efficient Collective-State Prediction with Decentralized JEPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28443)
*Alan-Barsag Gazzaev,Alexey Garvilov,Sergey Muravyov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Collective-State JEPA（CS-JEPA），一种用于群体机器人共享状态预测的循环联合嵌入预测架构。每个机器人仅通过局部观察和带宽受限的消息即可预测共同的未来集体状态，无需全局池化、目标编码器或记录的未来动作。实验表明，在多达108个机器人的多种拓扑和规模分布下，CS-JEPA在预测错误和机器人间一致性AUC上全面优于原始未来重构基线；在动作条件预测中，CS-JEPA将分支值MSE降低45.5%，候选分数皮尔逊相关提升0.1291。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多机器人群体系统中，关键挑战是每个机器人仅凭局部观测和带宽受限通信，能否预测相同的未来集体状态。现有方法通常依赖全局信息池化、共享时钟或记录的未来动作等集中式假设，这限制了在真实分布式场景中的部署能力。作者旨在探索一种无需这些假设、仅依赖局部信息即可实现一致集体状态预测的自监督框架。

Method: CS-JEPA是一种循环联合嵌入预测架构，其核心设计是：每个机器人的输出代表一个共同未来token场（common future token field）。部署时每个机器人使用16帧局部历史和一个每有向边64浮点数的循环消息，不使用全局池化、目标编码器、事件时钟或记录的未来动作。预训练阶段无需下游集体标签，之后通过岭回归探针（ridge probes）在6、12或24个全局标记episode上评估冻结表示。论文设计了两个前瞻性注册的实验：五种子对比CS-JEPA与原始未来重构；八种子对比动作条件预测器，后者接收每个候选四步计划后生成接收器局部预测表示。

Result: 在五种子前瞻性实验中（额外的9,607个训练参数），CS-JEPA在预测错误和机器人间一致性标签预算AUC上，于分布内、环形、互kNN和未见规模家族（最多108个机器人）中全面优于原始未来重构基线，效果在5/5种子中一致。在八个种子的动作条件实验中，CS-JEPA将分支值MSE降低45.5%，并在上下文中将候选分数皮尔逊相关性提升0.1291，两项效果在8/8种子中均有利，包括在未见N=32的情况下。

Conclusion: 共同未来JEPA目标可作为分布式群体预测中标签高效的原始方法（primitive），在拓扑和规模变化下保持稳健，并额外展示了对规划相关的价值估计能力。CS-JEPA证明了通过纯局部信息与受限通信实现一致集体状态预测的可行性，为群体机器人的决策规划提供了可扩展的自监督基础。

Abstract: Can every robot in a swarm predict the same future collective state from only local observations and bandwidth-limited messages? We formulate this as decentralized shared-state prediction and introduce Collective-State JEPA (CS-JEPA), a recurrent joint-embedding predictive architecture whose output at every robot represents one common future token field. At deployment, each robot uses a 16-frame local history and one 64-float recurrent message per directed edge; there is no global pooling, target encoder, episode clock, or recorded future action. After pretraining without downstream collective labels, frozen representations are evaluated with ridge probes fitted on 6, 12, or 24 globally labeled episodes. Against raw-future reconstruction with the same receiver anchor and deployment capacity but 9,607 additional training-only parameters, a prospectively registered five-seed follow-up improves prediction-error and inter-robot-agreement label-budget AUC on in-distribution, ring, mutual-kNN, and unseen-size families up to 108 robots. Every effect favors CS-JEPA in 5/5 outer seeds. In a separate sealed eight-seed follow-up, matched action-conditioned predictors receive each candidate four-step plan before producing receiver-local predictive representations. CS-JEPA reduces branch-value MSE by 45.5% and improves within-context candidate-score Pearson correlation by 0.1291, with both effects favorable in 8/8 seeds, including at unseen N=32. These results support common-future JEPA targets as a label-efficient primitive for decentralized swarm prediction under topology and size shift, with additional evidence of planning-relevant value estimation.

</details>


### [248] [Machines that know they are aging: a framework for hardware-aware autonomous intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28451)
*Cheng Siong Chin,Jianhua Zhang,Mohan Venkateshkumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"老化感知自主智能"(AAAI)框架，将硬件健康状态直接融入自主系统的推理、规划与任务执行中，以解决因硬件随时间老化引起的"不可知性崩溃"问题。该框架基于三大支柱：硬件自感知（利用物理失效模型持续估算电源、传感、内存和计算子系统的健康）、自适应推理（根据剩余硬件能力调整推理复杂度、规划范围与任务优先级）以及以生存为中心的智能（通过性能优化、资源节约和优雅退化跨任务分配剩余运行寿命）。AAAI不引入新硬件，而是将预测性维护、生命周期管理和硬件感知计算统一为闭环认知架构，适用于太空任务、海洋机器人和植入式医疗设备等不可达或安全关键环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统不可避免地会老化，但传统AI假设硬件保持原始状态。电池退化、传感器漂移、处理器时序误差积累和内存可靠性下降造成"假设能力"与"实际能力"之间日益扩大的不匹配，导致"不可知性崩溃"——任务失败源于累积的硬件退化而非单一组件故障。这种对硬件老化缺乏认知的现状构成了根本性风险。

Method: 提出AAAI框架，包含三个核心支柱：(1) 硬件自感知——利用物理失效模型持续估计电源、传感、内存和计算四大子系统的健康状态；(2) 自适应推理——根据剩余硬件能力动态调整推理复杂度、规划时间跨度和任务优先级；(3) 以生存为中心的智能——通过性能优化、资源节约和优雅退化三种策略将剩余运行寿命在任务目标间进行最优分配。框架不引入新硬件，统一了预测性维护、生命周期管理和硬件感知计算，形成闭环认知架构。

Result: 该框架能够使机器识别并响应自身老化，提升系统的弹性、延长运行寿命，并支持更安全、更优雅的任务完成方式。适用于太空任务、海洋机器人及植入式医疗设备等不可达或安全关键的应用场景。

Conclusion: 将硬件健康集成到认知架构中对在不可达或安全关键环境中运行的自主系统至关重要。AAAI通过统一的闭环架构弥合了硬件退化与AI决策之间的鸿沟，实现了从"假设硬件如新"到"感知硬件真实状态"的范式转变，大幅提升了自主系统的可靠性与寿命。

Abstract: Autonomous systems inevitably age, yet their artificial intelligence typically assumes hardware remains in its original condition. Batteries degrade, sensors drift, processors accumulate timing errors, and memory reliability declines, creating a growing mismatch between assumed and actual capability. This can lead to agnostic collapse, where mission failure arises from accumulated hardware degradation rather than a single component fault. We propose Aging-Aware Autonomous Intelligence (AAAI), a framework that integrates hardware health directly into reasoning, planning, and mission execution. AAAI is built on three pillars: hardware self-awareness, which continuously estimates the health of power, sensing, memory, and computation subsystems using physics-of-failure models; self-adaptive reasoning, which adjusts inference complexity, planning horizon, and task priorities according to remaining hardware capability; and survival-centric intelligence, which allocates remaining operational life across mission objectives through performance optimization, resource conservation, and graceful degradation. Rather than introducing new hardware, AAAI unifies prognostics, lifecycle management, and hardware-aware computing into a closed-loop cognitive architecture. We argue that such integration is essential for autonomous systems operating in inaccessible or safety-critical environments, including space missions, marine robotics, and implantable medical devices. By enabling machines to recognize and respond to their own aging, AAAI improves resilience, extends operational lifetime, and supports safer, more graceful mission completion.

</details>


### [249] [TEA-AgriVLN: Traversability Estimation Alarm for Agricultural Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28474)
*Xiaobei Zhao,Xingqi Lyu,Xin Chen,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TEA模块（Traversability Estimation and Alarm），通过估计相机图像的通行性并在地形可通行性与决策者预测动作不一致时触发重新思考，将AgriVLN方法在A2A基准上的成功率从0.47提升至0.54，导航误差从2.91m降至2.70m，达到农业VLN-CE领域的最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLN-CE方法从室内场景迁移到农业场景时面临一个关键挑战：室内场景中区域的通行性通常清晰可判（如木地板可通行、混凝土墙不可通行），而农业场景中通行性往往是模糊的（如未成熟的玉米地对机器狗可通行但对人类不可通行），这种模糊性导致现有通行性分类方法在农业场景中失效。

Method: 提出TEA模块，包含两个核心功能：(1) 通行性估计——对相机图像进行通行性预测，生成通行性地图；(2) 告警机制——当决策者（导航模型）预测的动作与通行性地图不一致时，触发重新思考（rethinking）过程，促使模型重新推理以避免执行不可通行的动作。该模块集成到AgriVLN主干架构中，构建TEA-AgriVLN方法。

Result: 在A2A基准上，TEA-AgriVLN将成功率（SR）从0.47提升至0.54，导航误差（NE）从2.91m降至2.70m，达到了农业VLN-CE领域的最先进性能。同时进行了消融实验和案例研究，讨论了TEA在不同地面类别和场景类别上的有效性与局限性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模农业场景中通行性的模糊性，并将通行性估计作为导航决策的外部监督信号，TEA模块有效解决了农业场景中通行性判断的歧义问题，验证了通行性感知（traversability-aware）的决策机制在农业VLN-CE任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires an agent to follow a natural language instruction, predicting a sequence of low-level actions to navigate a robot from a starting point to a target location. The A2A benchmark and the AgriVLN method pioneeringly extended VLN-CE from indoor scenes to agricultural scenes, while we observed a challenging distinction: In indoor scenes, whether a zone is traversable tends to be clear to classify, such as wood floors are traversable but concrete walls are not. In agricultural scenes, however, this issue tends to be ambiguous, such as an unripe cornfield might be traversable for a robotic dog but might be non-traversable for a human. To address this issue, we propose the TEA module, which estimates the traversability of the camera image, then alarm the decision-maker for rethinking when the predicted action does not align with the traversability map. We integrate it into the AgriVLN backbone to build our TEA-AgriVLN method. When evaluated on A2A, it improves Success Rate (SR) from 0.47 to 0.54 and Navigation Error (NE) from 2.91 m to 2.70 m, showing the state-of-the-art performance in the agricultural VLN-CE domain. We further implement the ablation studies and the case study, discussing the effectiveness and limitations of TEA on different ground categories and scene classes. Code: https://github.com/AlexTraveling/TEA-AgriVLN.

</details>


### [250] [X-NavDP: Generalizing Navigation Diffusion Policy to Novel Behavior and Embodiments with Group Q-score Reweighted Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28560)
*Tianyu Yang,Yiming Zeng,Wenzhe Cai,Yuqiang Yang,Jiaqi Peng,Hui Cheng,Jiangmiao Pang,Tai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GQRM（Group Q-score Reweighted Matching），一种数据高效的扩散策略RL后训练框架，用于跨形态视觉导航。通过自引导探索策略（保持预训练策略先验）和分组Q-score归一化机制（高效重加权分数匹配），将X-NavDP策略的成功率从61.20%提升至84.28%（仿真）及10%提升至65%（真实世界困难场景）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练导航扩散策略依赖大规模专家演示，这些数据通常由针对单一标称机器人的全知oracle规划器生成，限制了策略对多样形态和挑战性场景（如逃出死胡同、绕行长障碍物）的泛化能力。虽然可用RL后训练来弥补，但扩散策略的不可处理似然导致策略梯度不稳定，且策略探索效率低下，以往RL方法改进甚微。

Method: 提出GQRM框架，包含两个互补设计：(i) 自引导探索策略：通过行为扰动保留预训练策略先验，实现高效探索；(ii) 分组Q-score归一化机制：在每个状态上计算每条轨迹的值，实现高效的重加权分数匹配。在该框架下进行跨异构形态的分布式在线RL训练。

Result: 微调后的策略X-NavDP在跨形态视觉导航任务上达到SOTA，仿真环境中整体成功率从61.20%提升至84.28%，真实世界困难场景中成功率从10%提升至65%。

Conclusion: GQRM框架有效解决了扩散策略RL后训练中策略梯度不稳定和探索效率低下的问题，通过保留预训练先验的探索与Q-score重加权匹配的结合，显著提升了跨形态视觉导航性能，为扩散策略的后训练提供了一种数据高效的新范式。

Abstract: Pretraining navigation diffusion policies rely on large-scale expert demonstrations. These data are typically generated by a fully-informed oracle planner suited to a single nominal robot. This limits the policy's generalization to diverse embodiments and challenging scenarios (e.g., escaping dead ends or detouring long obstacles) that demand diverse local reactive behaviors with only onboard local observations. Post-training the policy with reinforcement learning (RL) offers a principled remedy. However, previous RL for diffusion approaches lead to only marginal improvements. This is because the intractable likelihood of diffusion policies renders policy gradients unstable in addition to inefficient policy exploration. To address these challenges, we propose a data-efficient diffusion RL post-training framework - GQRM (Group Q-score Reweighted Matching). Our framework introduces two complementary designs: (i) a self-bootstrapped exploration strategy with behavior perturbation that preserves the pretrained policy prior, and (ii) a group Q-score normalization mechanism that computes per-trajectory values on each state for efficient reweighted score matching. By conducting distributed online RL training across heterogeneous embodiments, the resulting fine-tuned policy, X-NavDP, achieves state-of-the-art cross-embodiment visual navigation performance, improving the overall success rate from 61.20% to 84.28% in simulation and 10% to 65% in real-world hard cases. The code and model are publicly available at https://yty-sky.github.io/x-navdp-project-page.

</details>


### [251] [FA-RDP: A Frequency-Adaptive Reactive Diffusion Policy for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28596)
*Lifeng Zhuo,Wendi Chen,Han Xue,Shirun Tang,Jun Lv,Cewu Lu,Chuan Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FA-RDP提出了一种频率自适应的反应式扩散策略，通过共享多频视觉-力Transformer预测高低频动作块，并利用学习的多模态指标动态选择低频多步采样（接触前）与高频单步采样（接触后），同时引入流形一致性蒸馏（MCD）在机器人动作流形上重参数化扩散网络，从而在接触丰富的操作任务中兼顾多模态保持与力反馈响应速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在接触丰富的操作任务中，同一回合的不同阶段存在矛盾的需求：接触前需要保留多个有效的动作模式（多模态），接触后则因几何约束与力限制使得解空间收窄，需要快速响应力反馈。然而标准扩散策略固定推理频率与采样步数，低频多步采样利于接触前多模态保持但反应慢，高频采样反应快却会导致接触前模式坍塌，二者存在根本性权衡。

Method: FA-RDP包含两部分核心设计：1）共享多频视觉-力Transformer，同时以低、高两种频率预测动作块，共享网络参数；2）学习得到的多模态指标动态切换策略——在接触前多模态性高时采用多步低频采样，当动作歧义性降低时切换为单步高频采样。此外引入Manifold Consistency Distillation（MCD），将扩散网络重参数化以在机器人动作流形上预测动作，同时保留基于DDPM的残差监督。

Result: 在三个接触丰富的操作任务上，FA-RDP取得了最高的成功率，同时在接触前保留了多样的轨迹模式，验证了其能同时兼顾多模态保持与力反馈反应能力。

Conclusion: 通过频率自适应的推理机制与流形一致性蒸馏，FA-RDP有效化解了扩散策略在多模态保持与快速反应之间的固有矛盾，为接触丰富操作任务提供了一种兼顾灵活性（灵活）与响应性（反应）的解决方案。

Abstract: In contact-rich manipulation, action multimodality and reactivity dominate different stages of a single episode. Before contact, multiple trajectories might be equally valid, making it important to preserve diverse action modes. After contact, geometric constraints and force limits narrow the solution space, while successful execution demands rapid responses to force feedback. However, standard diffusion policies use a fixed inference frequency and sampling steps throughout the episode, forcing a fundamental compromise: low-frequency, multi-step sampling better preserves pre-contact multimodality but responds slowly to force feedback, whereas high-frequency sampling improves reactivity but tends to collapse distinct pre-contact modes. To resolve this tradeoff, we present FA-RDP, a frequency-adaptive reactive diffusion policy. A shared multi-frequency visual-force Transformer predicts action chunks at both low and high frequencies, while a learned multimodality indicator dynamically selects multi-step low-frequency sampling before contact and one-step high-frequency sampling as action ambiguity decreases. We further introduce Manifold Consistency Distillation (MCD), which reparameterizes the diffusion network to predict actions on the robot action manifold while retaining DDPM-based residual supervision. Experiments on three contact-rich manipulation tasks show that FA-RDP achieves the highest success rate while preserving diverse pre-contact trajectory modes. Code and videos are available at https://fa-rdp.github.io.

</details>


### [252] [PAC-MAN: Perception-Aware CBF-RL for Whole-Body Safety in Humanoid Dodgeball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28623)
*Lizhi Yang,Junheng Li,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PAC-MAN是一个感知感知的CBF-RL框架，将控制障碍安全性与部署实际的机载感知相结合，用于全身人形机器人躲避球。在受控基准上接近特权状态oracle性能，并在真实Unitree G1机器人上以95%成功率实现零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然CBF在机器人安全控制中已被广泛使用，但其性能高度依赖准确的障碍物状态感知。在真实部署中，机器人只能依靠固定的机载摄像头获得不完美的感知信息，这导致CBF的安全保障能力大打折扣。作者旨在解决感知与安全控制之间的耦合问题，构建在真实感知条件下依然有效的安全躲避策略。

Method: 提出PAC-MAN框架：(1) 部署时策略仅使用头戴式摄像头的分割掩码深度作为输入；(2) 训练时利用CBF指导，计算到每个身体链接的间隙作为安全性约束；(3) 引入对抗性运动先验来正则化躲避反射。通过受控any-link接触基准评估，包含单次投掷和包含行走回站及恢复的部署循环两种场景。

Result: 该策略性能接近特权状态oracle。关键发现：CBF的有效性依赖于感知可观测性——Joint-CBF在精确球状态下表现最佳，但仅在固定摄像头观测下作为训练指导时会退化，而在球跟踪云台或特权运行时滤波下可以恢复。部署的轻量Link-CBF策略在真实Unitree G1上以零样本方式运行，容忍不完美感知，95%的投掷成功躲避，并能用语义分割区分不同球。

Conclusion: 固定机载摄像头单独即可满足躲避需求，安全性屏障结构的可用性与感知可观测性密切相关。通过选择适当的CBF形式（Link-CBF），可以在真实世界中实现零样本部署，即便面对不完美感知也能保持高成功率，验证了感知感知的安全学习框架的实用价值。

Abstract: We present PAC-MAN, a perception-aware CBF-RL framework that couples control-barrier safety with deployment-realistic onboard sensing for whole-body humanoid dodgeball. The deployed policy sees the ball only as segmentation-masked depth from a head-mounted camera, while training-time CBF guidance represents clearance to every body link, and an adversarial motion prior regularizes the resulting evasive reflexes. We evaluate on a controlled any-link contact benchmark with seeded throws in two regimes: single throws and a deployment loop in which the robot walks back to its station and recovers between throws. On this benchmark, the policy comes within a few points of a privileged state oracle: a fixed onboard camera alone is adequate for evasion. We find that usable barrier structure depends on perceptual observability: Joint-CBF gives the best performance with accurate ball states, degrades under fixed-camera observations when used only as training guidance, and recovers with a ball-tracking gimbal or privileged runtime filter. We therefore deploy a lightweight Link-CBF policy zero-shot on the Unitree G1 in the real world, where it tolerates imperfect perception, succeeds on 95% of throws, and uses semantic segmentation to dodge different balls.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [253] [Recursive transformers for semiconductor thermo-mechanical reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27251)
*Kart-leong Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并系统评估了三种递归Transformer架构（Tiny Recursive Model、Depth Recursive transformer和简单递归transformer），用于替代昂贵的第一性原理仿真，解决工程设计中低维小数据集下传统Transformer过参数化导致的过拟合问题。通过在半导体封装热-机械可靠性分析和Laplace PDE电容场求解两个低维工程任务上的验证，表明递归权重共享Transformer在预测精度、参数效率和计算成本之间提供了有效且可泛化的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer架构在工程设计中典型的小规模、低维数据集上往往参数过多（over-parameterized），导致过拟合而非精度提升，同时带来不必要的内存和计算开销。这促使研究者探索将更多计算而非更多可学习参数投入模型的架构方向。

Method: 提出并比较三种递归Transformer范式：a) Tiny Recursive Model、b) 所提出的Depth Recursive transformer、c) 简单递归transformer。从预测性能（Recall、MRR）、参数量和FLOPs三个方面进行硬件感知的系统评估，并在两个低维工程预测任务上验证：先进半导体封装的热-机械可靠性分析（FEA热循环应力和翘曲评估）以及Laplace PDE迭代数值求解器的电容场计算。

Result: 递归权重共享Transformer在预测精度、参数效率和计算成本之间提供了有效且可泛化的权衡，适用于小数据工程的代理建模。Depth Recursive transformer在资源受限场景下表现出优越的性能-参数量-计算量平衡。

Conclusion: 在工程设计的低维小数据场景中，应倾向于选择通过递归权重共享来增加计算深度而非增加可学习参数量的架构。该原则在半导体封装可靠性分析和PDE数值求解两个任务上均得到验证，为资源受限场景下递归Transformer架构的选择提供了实用设计指南。

Abstract: Transformer-based surrogate models are increasingly used to replace expensive first-principles simulation in engineering design. But conventional transformer architectures are often over parameterized for the small, low-dimensional datasets typical of engineering design spaces, where large simulation data is expensive to generate. Under these conditions, excess parameter capacity leads to overfitting rather than improved accuracy, while also incurring unnecessary memory and compute overhead. This motivates a shift towards architectures that focus on additional compute rather than additional learnable parameters. This paper presents a hardware-aware evaluation of three recursive transformer paradigms for surrogate thermo-mechanical analysis of advanced packages: a)Tiny Recursive Model, b) our proposed Depth Recursive transformer, c) and a simple recursive transformer. We systematically compare their predictive performance (Recall, Mean Reciprocal Rank), parameter count, computational complexity (FLOPs), providing practical design guidelines for selecting recursive transformer architectures under resource-constrained scenarios. We validate this principle on two low-dimensional engineering prediction tasks: 1) thermo-mechanical reliability analysis of advanced semiconductor packages, where stress and warpage from thermal cycling must be evaluated repeatedly across a design-of-experiments sweep under costly finite element analysis (FEA). 2) Laplace PDE iterative numerical solver for capacitance field. Overall, recursive weight-sharing transformers provide an effective and generalizable trade-off between prediction accuracy, parameter efficiency, and computational cost for small data engineering surrogate modeling.

</details>


### [254] [Regularizing modality contribution drift in multimodal continual learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27260)
*Zhen Zhang,Jielei Chu,Bin Liu,Tianrui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出模态贡献漂移（MCD）概念，揭示了多模态持续学习（MMCL）中现有方法忽视模态相对贡献变化导致遗忘的问题，并提出CMCDR正则化方法来保持模态贡献结构，包含基于回放和无回放两个版本，在多项任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMCL方法主要关注跨模态表示对齐或语义相似性，但忽视了在增量任务中各个模态的相对贡献及其交互是否保持稳定。这种决策层面的模态贡献漂移未被显式建模，导致即便使用现有方法仍存在灾难性遗忘。

Method: 首先提出MCD分数来量化模态贡献漂移，该分数通过在受控的模态子集干预下结合贡献强度变化和相对依赖变化来计算。接着提出CMCDR正则化方法，包含两个版本：基于回放版本利用模态子集干预作为诊断探针，在存储的旧样本上比较当前模型与冻结的旧模型之间的贡献轮廓并约束变化；无回放版本使用当前任务样本作为探针，蒸馏冻结模型的旧任务贡献响应来正则化观测到的贡献轮廓。

Result: 在多模态类增量学习和持续视觉问答任务上的实验验证了CMCDR的通用性和有效性，实验结果支持了理论和经验分析中关于现有MMCL方法无法可靠缓解模态贡献漂移的结论。

Conclusion: 保留模态贡献结构是缓解多模态持续学习遗忘的关键，CMCDR通过显式正则化模态特定和交互贡献的变化，能够在有或没有旧样例的情况下有效抑制模态贡献漂移，提升持续学习性能。

Abstract: Multimodal continual learning (MMCL) aims to learn emerging knowledge from multimodal data while preserving knowledge. To mitigate forgetting, current MMCL methods usually focus on cross-modal representation alignment or semantic similarity, but they overlook whether the relative contributions of individual modalities and their interactions remain stable across incremental tasks. We term this decision-level shift Modality Contribution Drift (MCD) and quantify it with the MCD score, which combines contribution-strength and relative-reliance changes under controlled interventions on modality subsets. Theoretical and empirical analyses further explain why current MMCL methods cannot reliably mitigate this drift. To this end, we propose Continual Modality Contribution Drift Regularization (CMCDR), which preserves the modality contribution structure of previously learned tasks. Since MMCL settings differ in whether old exemplars are available, CMCDR includes both replay-based and replay-free versions. The replay-based version uses modality-subset interventions as diagnostic probes on stored old samples, compares their contribution profiles between the current model and a frozen previous model, and constrains changes in old-sample modality-specific and interaction contributions. The replay-free version uses current-task samples as probes and distills the frozen model's old-task contribution responses, thereby regularizing the observed contribution profile without exemplars. Experiments on multimodal class-incremental learning and continual visual question answering validate the generality and effectiveness of CMCDR.

</details>


### [255] [DoTime: A Synthetic Benchmark Generator for Interventional and Counterfactual Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27263)
*Dennis Thumm,Billy Tim Anthony,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DoTime，一个开源、可扩展、理论严谨的多元时间序列结构因果模型(TSCM)生成器，支持干预和反事实采样，并附带四个冻结评估套件和参考基线实现。核心发现：干预训练（prior-fitted network, PFN）相比同等容量的观测模型，在方向准确率上具有可测量的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列因果推断基准大多是基于观测数据的、规模较小或领域特定，缺少对干预和反事实估计的支持——这恰恰是医疗、政策评估和气候科学等关键领域最需要的。因此需要一个开放、可扩展且理论上扎实的基准生成器来填补这一空白。

Method: DoTime通过以下机制实现：(1) 生成多元时间序列结构因果模型(TSCM)并注入干预；(2) 支持连续时间干预窗口；(3) 提供带积极性保护(positivity guard)的反事实采样模式；(4) 实现机制切换SCM作为中断时间序列(ITS)的严格推广；(5) 通过切换SCM参数构造非平稳动态；(6) 在评估窗口内嵌入确定性斜坡和正弦干预曲线以放置趋势和结构断点。同时提供四个冻结评估套件（含10万条轨迹的规模快照和8种命名识别结构、精确的成对干预轨迹与共享噪声反事实），以及参考基线和评估框架。作为因果基础模型基准，使用三组训练种子对干预训练PFN与同容量观测模型进行对比。

Result: 在结构匹配的留出片段评估中，干预先验拟合网络(PFN)的准确率差距在所有结构、轨迹长度和训练种子下均为正数，验证了干预训练相比同等容量观测模型能带来可测量的方向准确率优势。

Conclusion: DoTime是一个可作为干预-反事实时间序列因果推理通用基准的生成器，兼具理论严谨性和实际可扩展性，并证明其适合作为因果基础模型的先验数据来源。文章还提出了一个可证伪的主张：干预训练带来比观测模型更优的方向准确率优势。

Abstract: Most benchmarks for causal inference over time series are observational, small, or domain-specific, leaving interventional and counterfactual estimation under-served exactly where it matters most, such as in healthcare, policy evaluation, and climate science. We introduce \textbf{DoTime}, an open, scalable, and theoretically grounded generator of multivariate temporal structural causal models (TSCMs) with interventions, released as the \code{dotime} PyPI package together with four frozen evaluation suites. Beyond existing work, it adds capabilities absent from prior generators: continuous-time intervention \emph{windows}, counterfactual sampling modes with a positivity guard, regime-switching SCMs as a strict generalization of interrupted time series, non-stationary dynamics by construction with switching SCM parameters, and deterministic ramp and sinusoidal intervention profiles that place trends and structural breaks \emph{inside} the evaluation window. Moreover, it demonstrates the suitability of the generator as a prior for a causal foundation model reference implementation. The released suites span a training-scale snapshot of $100{,}000$ trajectories and eight named identification structures, each with exact ground truth: paired interventional trajectories from the same SCM throughout, and shared-noise counterfactuals in the continuous-time suite. We ship reference baseline implementations with an evaluation harness, and pose a falsifiable claim: interventional training buys a measurable direction-accuracy advantage over an observational model of identical capacity. It is tested across three training seeds per arm. Under structure-matched evaluation on held-out episodes, the interventional prior-fitted network's (PFN) gap is positive in every structure, trajectory length, and seed tested.

</details>


### [256] [PlatformBid: An Auto-Bidding Benchmark from a Unified Advertising Platform's Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27265)
*Shengtian Yang,Yewen Li,Peng Jiang,Zhiyi Lyu,Bo An,Peng Jiang,Qingpeng Cai,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PlatformBid，首个从统一广告平台视角设计的自动出价综合基准，涵盖同质竞争、异质竞争和促销竞争三种真实场景，并提出了基于流匹配的新方法BidFlow，在线实验显示目标成本提升0.68%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动出价算法仅从DSP（需求方平台）角度出发，只关注最大化广告主转化率，但当前大型广告平台（如社交媒体和电商公司）已内部整合SSP、DSP和Ad Exchange功能。从这些平台视角来看，自动出价算法的目标不仅是最大化广告主转化，还要最大化平台总营收。然而，目前缺乏以平台为中心的评估框架，亟需推进自动出价研究。

Method: 提出PlatformBid综合基准，定义了三种代表性设置：(1)同质竞争——所有广告主使用相同算法；(2)异质竞争——广告主使用多样化的算法策略；(3)促销竞争——部分广告主在促销活动（如黑色星期五）期间增加预算。系统评估了现有自动出价方法（经典控制方法、RL方法、近期生成方法）。此外，提出基于流匹配的新方法BidFlow，利用流匹配方法的表达性策略表示来有效处理动态竞争环境。

Result: 在快手平台上的在线实验显示，BidFlow实现了+0.68%的目标成本改进，为PlatformBid基准的离线-在线一致性提供了部署证据。

Conclusion: PlatformBid是首个统一广告平台视角下的自动出价综合基准，填补了平台中心评估框架的空白，并通过提出的BidFlow方法及在线部署验证证明了该基准的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Real-time bidding is central to computational advertising, comprising three elements: Supply Side Platform (SSP) selling ad impressions, Demand Side Platform (DSP) bidding for advertisers, and Ad Exchange conducting auctions between them. Traditional auto-bidding algorithms focus solely on the DSP side, maximizing advertiser conversions by adjusting bids against competitors. However, current big ad platforms, such as social media and e-commerce companies, now integrate SSP, DSP, and Ad Exchange functions internally. From such ad platforms' perspective, the goal of the auto-bidding algorithms is not only to maximize the advertisers' conversions, but also the total revenue of the platform. Given the lack of platform-centric evaluation frameworks and the pressing need to advance auto-bidding research, we propose PlatformBid - the first comprehensive benchmark designed from a unified ad platform's perspective. To accurately reflect the real-world auto-bidding scenarios, we define three representative settings: (1) homogeneous competition with identical algorithms across advertisers, (2) heterogeneous competition with diverse algorithmic strategies, and (3) promotional competition where some advertisers surge budgets for boosting sales during promotional events like Black Friday. We systematically evaluate a broad spectrum of existing auto-bidding methods across these settings, encompassing classical control methods, RL-based methods, and recent generative methods. Besides these methods, we further propose a novel auto-bidding method based on flow-matching, termed BidFlow, which leverages the flow-matching method's expressive policy representation to effectively handle dynamic competitive environments. Online experiments on Kuaishou further show a +0.68\% improvement in target cost, providing deployment evidence for the offline-online consistency of PlatformBid.

</details>


### [257] [Beyond KV Reconstruction: Functional Reconstruction for MLA Draft Models in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27269)
*Weiye Shi,Fanxu Meng,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端(E2E)的功能重构方法，通过优化转换后的MLA注意力模块来复现原始MHA/GQA注意力的输出响应，从而解决直接MHA/GQA到MLA转换导致的自回归生成中草稿接受率显著下降的问题。在192个模型-转换器-后端-方法-任务配置上的评估显示，该方法在64个匹配任务单元中37个显著提升了接受率，仅1个下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多头潜在注意力(MLA)通过紧凑的潜在状态替代不断增长的KV缓存，对长上下文LLM推理越来越重要。然而大多数开源模型使用MHA/GQA，需要转换才能获得MLA的缓存效率。研究发现直接转换会因低秩分解和RoPE处理引入注意力函数误差，这种误差可能对独立生成影响不大，但会显著降低投机解码中草稿标记的接受率，因为草稿和目标验证之间的一致性大幅下降。

Method: 将MLA草稿构建定义为功能重构(functional reconstruction)而非缓存压缩(cache compression)。端到端(E2E)方法优化每个转换后的MLA注意力模块，使其在校准隐藏状态上复现原始MHA/GQA对应模块的后输出投影(post-output-projection)响应。该方法与具体转换器无关，作为转换后的后处理步骤，保留转换后的缓存和推理图，且不需要验证器的logits或监督信号。

Result: 在192个配置上评估，涵盖4个Llama/Qwen草稿-目标对、TransMLA和MHA2MLA两种转换器、HF和vLLM两个后端、4个200提示词任务。以0.5个百分点为报告容差，功能重构在64个匹配任务单元中37个显著提升接受率，26个基本不变，仅1个显著下降。

Conclusion: 功能重构方法能在无需重新训练、无需验证器监督的前提下，有效恢复MHA/GQA到MLA转换后的投机解码草稿接受率，为现有模型向MLA架构的高效转换提供实用路径。

Abstract: Multi-head latent attention (MLA) is increasingly important for long-context LLM inference because compact latent states replace the growing key-value (KV) cache and reduce decoding memory traffic. Yet most capable open checkpoints use multi-head or grouped-query attention (MHA/GQA), so conversion is needed to obtain MLA's cache efficiency without retraining from scratch. Speculative decoding offers complementary acceleration, but its speedup depends on agreement between draft proposals and target verification. We find that direct MHA/GQA-to-MLA conversion can sharply reduce this agreement: low-rank factorization and RoPE handling introduce attention-function errors that may be tolerable for standalone generation but substantially lower draft-token acceptance. We therefore formulate MLA draft construction as functional reconstruction rather than cache compression. Our end-to-end (E2E) method optimizes each converted MLA attention module to reproduce the post-output-projection response of its original MHA/GQA counterpart on calibration hidden states. This converter-agnostic post-conversion procedure preserves the converted cache and inference graph and requires neither verifier logits nor verifier supervision. We evaluate 192 model-converter-backend-method-task configurations spanning four Llama/Qwen draft-target pairs, TransMLA and MHA2MLA, HF and vLLM, and four 200-prompt tasks. With a 0.5-percentage-point reporting tolerance, Functional Reconstruction materially improves acceptance in 37 of 64 matched task cells, leaves 26 practically unchanged, and materially decreases one. Code and evaluation artifacts are available at https://github.com/swyhahaha/FunctionalMLA.

</details>


### [258] [RLPF: Reinforcement Learning from Performance Feedback for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27271)
*Huihao Jing,Haozhe Cui,Wenbin Hu,Shaojin Chen,Haochen Shi,Changxuan Fan,Yuxuan Liu,Hanyu Yang,Sirui Zhang,Ziyi Chen,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RLPF（基于性能反馈的强化学习），将执行结果转化为分级奖励：失败程序按执行进度排序，正确程序按相对于基线到专家参考的改进程度排序，从而训练代码智能体偏好更快的正确实现。在PerfCodeBench上将Qwen3-32B的正确可运行率从11.1%提升至54.6%，相对效率从8.1%提升至38.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码模型训练虽引入执行反馈，但训练信号仅止于正确性。两个程序可以通过相同测试却在运行时间上差异巨大，对于系统代码尤其如此。运行时间作为奖励是脆弱的：仅在程序正确后才有意义、跨任务方差大、且当大多数采样程序无法编译运行时几乎不提供指导。

Method: 提出RLPF（Reinforcement Learning from Performance Feedback），将执行结果转化为分级奖励。具体分两阶段：失败程序按执行进度排序以提供正确性之前的反馈；正确程序按从基线到专家参考的相对改进排序以提供正确性之后的性能敏感反馈。

Result: 在PerfCodeBench上，RLPF微调Qwen3-32B将正确可运行解从11.1%提升到54.6%，相对效率从8.1%提升到38.6%。训练后的模型与更强的开源系统竞争，优化行为适度迁移到EffiBench-X。模型生成的参考提供有用但较弱的监督；完整复合奖励比仅正确性或仅运行时间基线更可靠。

Conclusion: 代码智能体不仅可以被训练来通过测试，还可以被训练来优化其编写的程序，表明性能反馈可以作为代码模型训练的有效信号。

Abstract: Code models are increasingly trained with execution feedback, but most training signals still stop at correctness. This leaves an important gap for systems code: two programs can pass the same tests while differing greatly in runtime. We study how to train code agents to prefer faster correct implementations, rather than treating efficiency only as an evaluation metric. The key difficulty is that runtime is a fragile reward. It is meaningful only after a program is correct, varies across tasks, and gives little guidance when most sampled programs fail to compile or run. We propose \textbf{RLPF}, reinforcement learning from performance feedback, which turns execution outcomes into a staged reward. Failed programs are ordered by execution progress, while correct programs are ranked by their relative improvement from the baseline toward the expert reference. This gives useful feedback before correctness and performance-sensitive feedback after correctness. Fine-tuning Qwen3-32B with RLPF on PerfCodeBench raises correct-and-runnable solutions from $11.1\%$ to $54.6\%$ and improves relative efficiency from $8.1\%$ to $38.6\%$. The trained model becomes competitive with stronger open-weight systems, and its optimization behavior transfers modestly to EffiBench-X. Additional studies show that model-generated references provide useful but weaker supervision, and that the full composite reward is more reliable than correctness-only or runtime-only baselines. These results suggest that code agents can be trained not only to pass tests, but also to optimize the programs they write.

</details>


### [259] [SDO: Structure-Aware Data Organization for Efficient LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27273)
*Jinliang Gao,Ning Yang,Hai Wang,Baili Xiao,Pin Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SDO（结构感知数据组织）框架，通过暴露驱动反馈机制，在训练过程中动态组织小批量组成和样本暴露，利用外部嵌入的KNN邻域遍历实现局部感知批处理，并通过每轮次调整样本采样概率实现暴露均衡调度，在SFT、DPO和GRPO中加速收敛并提升各题型准确率的平衡性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的后期训练成本高昂，现有效率优化主要关注样本选择或训练调度，但数据组织本身被视为静态预处理步骤：基于嵌入的分组方法在训练前构建固定划分，无法适应优化过程中样本暴露的演化。这导致所有样本获得相似的暴露程度，尽管它们有不同的优化需求，造成部分样本的冗余更新而另一些样本优化不足。

Method: SDO是一个即插即用的数据组织框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 局部感知批处理（locality-aware batching）：在每个epoch内，通过KNN邻域遍历形成连贯的小批量；(2) 暴露均衡调度（exposure-balanced scheduling）：跨epoch记录每个样本的参与次数，降低过度暴露样本的采样概率以保持长期覆盖率。SDO基于冻结的外部嵌入逐epoch运行，避免了模型预热训练的开销。

Result: 在SFT、DPO和GRPO三种训练范式下，SDO均加速了收敛，最大的收益出现在训练的早中期阶段，产生了更连贯的梯度分布，以及各问题类型之间更均衡的准确率，且不会永久排除训练样本。

Conclusion: SDO通过将数据组织从静态预处理转变为暴露驱动的动态过程，有效解决了样本暴露不均的问题，为LLM后期训练提供了一种无额外训练开销的性能提升方案。

Abstract: Post-training of large language models is expensive, and existing efficiency improvements mainly focus on selecting informative samples or designing training schedules. However, data organization itself is usually treated as a static preprocessing step: embedding-based grouping methods construct fixed partitions before training and cannot adapt to the evolving sample exposure during optimization. As a result, all samples receive similar exposure despite their different optimization needs, leading to redundant updates for some samples while leaving others under-optimized. To address this problem, we propose SDO (Structure-Aware Data Organization), a plug-and-play data organization framework with an exposure-driven feedback mechanism that organizes mini-batch composition and sample exposure according to representation-space structure. SDO operates epoch by epoch on frozen external embeddings, avoiding model warm-up training overhead: within each epoch, locality-aware batching forms coherent mini-batches via KNN neighborhood traversal; across epochs, exposure-balanced scheduling records per-sample participation and reduces the sampling probability of over-exposed samples to preserve long-term coverage. Across SFT, DPO, and GRPO, SDO accelerates convergence, with the largest gains observed in the early-to-mid phase, producing more coherent gradients and more balanced accuracy across question types without permanently excluding training samples.

</details>


### [260] [Rethinking EEG-Based Disease Diagnosis: Decoupling Instance Representation Learning from Subject-Level Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27274)
*Zhiyuan Ma,Zeyuan Li,Zhiyi Lu,Jiacheng Hao,Youlang Du,Zhen Jiang,Xinche Zhang,Yuhao Sun,Sen Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BridgeMIL，一个两阶段框架，将实例表示学习与受试者级监督解耦，用于基于EEG的疾病诊断，在三个EEG疾病数据集和五个主干网络上达到最高平均准确率76.57%，比最强基线高4.28个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EEG疾病诊断流水线将记录分段后继承受试者标签训练实例级分类器，假设所有实例提供同等可靠的诊断证据，这一假设不成立。MIL虽避免继承标签，但EEG数据集受试者数远少于实例数，限制了端到端MIL学到的表示质量。

Method: BridgeMIL采用两阶段框架：(1) 第一阶段在无继承实例标签下预训练编码器，通过对齐时间邻近窗口和独立采样的受试者内子袋，辅以方差和协方差正则化防止表示坍缩并降低冗余，无需负样本对；(2) 第二阶段将编码器迁移到基于注意力的MIL聚合器，仅在受试者级预测上施加监督，并通过特征保留限制表示漂移。

Result: 在三个EEG疾病数据集和五个代表性主干的14/15设置中取得最高平均准确率，总体平均准确率76.57%，比最强基线高4.28个百分点。分析显示实例间继承标签可靠性存在显著差异，性能对受试者稀缺比实例稀缺更敏感，表示空间呈现更结构化的受试者级聚类和更清晰的诊断类别分离。

Conclusion: 对齐监督信号与受试者级预测目标至关重要，从丰富的EEG实例中学习时不应将疾病标签分配给单个实例。BridgeMIL通过解耦实例表示学习与受试者级监督，有效解决了EEG数据中受试者稀缺问题，验证了MIL在EEG疾病诊断中的有效性。

Abstract: EEG-based disease diagnosis requires one prediction per subject, yet common pipelines segment recordings into short instances, inherit the subject label for every instance, and train instance-level classifiers. This assumes that all instances provide equally reliable diagnostic evidence. Multiple instance learning (MIL) avoids inherited labels by treating each subject as a bag. However, EEG datasets contain far fewer subjects than instances, which can limit the quality of the representations learned by end-to-end MIL. We propose BridgeMIL, a two-stage framework that decouples instance representation learning from subject-level supervision. Stage 1 pretrains the encoder without inherited instance labels by aligning temporally nearby windows and independently sampled within-subject sub-bags. Variance and covariance regularization prevent collapse and reduce redundancy without negative pairs. Stage 2 transfers the encoder to an attention-based MIL aggregator, applies supervision only to subject predictions, and limits representation drift through feature retention. Across three EEG disease datasets and five representative backbones, BridgeMIL attains the highest mean accuracy in 14 of 15 dataset-backbone settings and an overall mean accuracy of 76.57%, 4.28 percentage points higher than the strongest baseline. Further analyses reveal substantial variation in inherited-label reliability across instances, greater performance sensitivity to subject scarcity than to instance scarcity, and a more structured representation space with distinct subject-wise clusters and improved separation between diagnostic classes. Together, these findings underscore the importance of aligning supervision with the subject-level prediction objective while learning from abundant EEG instances without assigning disease labels to individual instances.

</details>


### [261] [Flat Score, Amplified Failures: How the Error Budget Masks Damage in Quantized LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27275)
*Jiwon Jang,Kisu Yang,Heuiseok Lim,Hyunwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验了4比特权重量化对多轮工具调用智能体的影响。在τ²-bench基准上，标准指标显示量化几乎无损（所有单元的变化均不显著），但过程分析揭示量化将模型已有的失败（如工具名幻觉）放大了最多2.5倍（每任务+17.6分），且未创造新失败类型。分数持平是因为基准的十个错误预算吸收了额外失败；将预算缩减至两个错误时，仅在有量化损伤的单元重新暴露17分差距。作者建议除了任务奖励外，同时报告每通道错误率和缩减预算下的成功率两个诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4比特权重量化被广泛报道为近乎无损，但这一结论主要基于标准评估指标。随着多轮工具调用智能体成为大模型最重要的应用场景，需要严格检验量化在该场景下的真实影响，特别是标准指标是否掩盖了过程层面的损伤。

Method: 在τ²-bench基准上，对两个开放权重模型家族的稠密和MoE变体、两个领域共八个单元（每单元456个episode）进行16-bit、8-bit和4-bit权重量化实验。使用多重比较校正评估标准指标变化，进行等价性检验，并引入过程分析：按通道统计错误率、分析失败集的一致性（秩相关和新增事件比例）、通过缩减错误预算（从十到二）暴露被掩盖的分数差距，以及使用针对性的错误修复提示验证损伤定位。

Result: 标准指标上空格（cell）均无显著变化，误差预算内的变化不超过±7.5分。但过程层面，量化将已有失败放大最多2.5倍（+17.6点/任务），主要在电信领域的工具名幻觉（零售领域实体错误有相同方向趋势），几乎不产生新失败（失败集秩相关≥0.94，仅0.18%新事件）。十错误预算掩盖了差距；缩减至两错误后，仅在量化增加错误量的单元重新暴露17分差距。错误修复提示恰好仅修复量化损伤所在的位置。

Conclusion: 标准任务奖励指标会掩盖量化的真实代价：量化不创造新失败而是放大已有失败，基准的错误预算越多，损伤越容易被掩盖。建议在报告任务奖励的同时，报告每通道错误率和缩减预算下的成功率作为补充诊断，以揭示量化对工具调用智能体的真实影响。

Abstract: Post-training quantization to 4-bit weights is widely reported to be nearly lossless. We test this claim for multi-turn, tool-calling agents, where it now matters most. On $τ^2$-bench, across two open-weight model families in dense and MoE variants and two domains (eight cells, 456 episodes each, at 16-, 8-, and 4-bit weights), quantization indeed looks free on the standard metric. No cell shows a score change that survives multiple-comparison correction, and in the cell that carries the largest process damage, equivalence testing bounds the change within $\pm$7.5 points. The process tells a different story. Quantization amplifies the failure the model already exhibits at full precision (tool-name hallucination in telecom, with the same directional trend in retail entity errors) by up to 2.5$\times$ in volume (+17.6 points per task), while creating essentially no new failures. The failure set is the same at every precision (rank correlation $\geq$ 0.94, 0.18% novel events). The score stays flat because the benchmark's ten-error budget absorbs the extra failures. Shrinking the budget to two errors re-exposes a score gap of 17 points, and it does so only in the one cell where quantization added error volume, exactly as the masking account predicts. A targeted error-repair prompt, run for five telecom models at every precision, removes the damage exactly and only where it lives. Both diagnostics, the per-channel error rate and success under a shrinking budget, come from logs benchmarks already collect; we suggest reporting them alongside task reward.

</details>


### [262] [The Kinetics of Training: A Driven-Nucleation Rate Law for Emergence, Plasticity Loss, and Circuit Control in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27281)
*Lei Dong*

Main category: cs.LG
